<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    
<meta http-equiv="content-type" content="text/html; charset=UTF-8" />
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/leaflet@1.9.3/dist/leaflet.js"></script>
    <script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.7.1.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.2.2/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
    <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/Leaflet.awesome-markers/2.0.2/leaflet.awesome-markers.js"></script>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/leaflet@1.9.3/dist/leaflet.css"/>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.2.2/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css"/>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://netdna.bootstrapcdn.com/bootstrap/3.0.0/css/bootstrap-glyphicons.css"/>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@fortawesome/fontawesome-free@6.2.0/css/all.min.css"/>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/Leaflet.awesome-markers/2.0.2/leaflet.awesome-markers.css"/>
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/python-visualization/folium/folium/templates/leaflet.awesome.rotate.min.css"/>
    
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,
                initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1.0, user-scalable=no" />
            <style>
                #map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292 {
                    position: relative;
                    width: 100.0%;
                    height: 100.0%;
                    left: 0.0%;
                    top: 0.0%;
                }
                .leaflet-container { font-size: 1rem; }
            </style>

<style>html, body {
                width: 100%;
                height: 100%;
                margin: 0;
                padding: 0;
            }
            </style>

<style>#map {
                position:absolute;
                top:0;
                bottom:0;
                right:0;
                left:0;
                }
            </style>

<script>
                L_NO_TOUCH = false;
                L_DISABLE_3D = false;
            </script>

        
</head>
<body>
    
    
<link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
<style>
:root {
  --c-primary: #2E7D32;
  --c-primary-dark: #235f27;
  --c-secondary: #1565C0;
  --c-accent: #FB8C00;
  --c-bg: #ffffff;
  --c-panel: #F7F9FC;
  --c-border: #E3E8ED;
  --c-text: #16262E;
  --c-text-mute: #5B6B73;
  --radius: 14px;
  --shadow: 0 6px 24px rgba(16, 40, 55, 0.12);
  font-family: -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
}
* { box-sizing: border-box; }
body { margin: 0; }
.leaflet-container { background: #0B2B3C; }

/* ---------------- Top nav ---------------- */
#gis-topnav {
  position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; height: 64px; z-index: 10010;
  background: var(--c-bg); box-shadow: 0 2px 10px rgba(16,40,55,0.08);
  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between;
  padding: 0 18px; font-family: inherit;
}
#gis-topnav .nav-left, #gis-topnav .nav-right { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; flex: 1; }
#gis-topnav .nav-right { justify-content: flex-end; }
#gis-topnav .nav-title { flex: 2; text-align: center; font-weight: 700; font-size: 17px; color: var(--c-text); letter-spacing: 0.01em; }
#gis-topnav button {
  display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; border: none; background: transparent; cursor: pointer;
  font-size: 13px; color: var(--c-text); padding: 8px 10px; border-radius: 8px; font-family: inherit;
}
#gis-topnav button:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
#gis-topnav .icon { font-size: 15px; }

/* ---------------- Map area ---------------- */
#gis-map-wrap { position: fixed; top: 64px; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; transition: left 0.25s ease; }
#gis-map-wrap.sidebar-open { left: 320px; }

/* ---------------- Left "Layers" sidebar ---------------- */
#gis-left-sidebar {
  position: fixed; top: 64px; left: 0; bottom: 0; width: 320px; z-index: 10005;
  padding: 14px; overflow-y: auto; transform: translateX(-100%); transition: transform 0.25s ease;
  background: transparent;
}
#gis-left-sidebar.open { transform: translateX(0); }
.gis-card {
  background: var(--c-bg); border-radius: var(--radius); box-shadow: var(--shadow); margin-bottom: 12px; overflow: hidden;
}
.gis-search-card { padding: 10px 12px; }
.gis-search-card input {
  width: 100%; box-sizing: border-box; padding: 9px 12px; font-size: 13px; border: 1px solid var(--c-border);
  border-radius: 10px; background: var(--c-panel); color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-accordion-header {
  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 13px 14px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-accordion-header:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
.gis-accordion-chevron { transition: transform 0.15s ease; color: var(--c-text-mute); font-size: 11px; }
.gis-accordion.open .gis-accordion-chevron { transform: rotate(90deg); }
.gis-accordion-body { max-height: 0; overflow: hidden; transition: max-height 0.2s ease; }
.gis-accordion.open .gis-accordion-body { max-height: 2000px; }
.gis-accordion-body-inner { padding: 0 14px 14px; }
.gis-placeholder { font-size: 12px; color: var(--c-text-mute); padding: 6px 0 2px; font-style: italic; }
.gis-layer-toggle-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--c-text); padding: 4px 2px; cursor: pointer; }
.gis-layer-swatch { width: 16px; height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; flex-shrink: 0; }
.gis-layer-count { margin-left: auto; font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--c-text-mute); background: #fff; border: 1px solid var(--c-border); border-radius: 20px; padding: 1px 8px; }

.gis-toolbar { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.gis-toolbar button {
  flex: 1 1 auto; font-size: 11px; padding: 6px 8px; border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap;
  border: 1px solid var(--c-border); background: var(--c-panel); color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-toolbar button:hover { background: #ecf1f0; }
.gis-toolbar button.primary { background: var(--c-primary); color: #fff; border-color: var(--c-primary); }
.gis-toolbar button.primary:hover { background: var(--c-primary-dark); }
.gis-stats { font-size: 11px; color: var(--c-text-mute); margin: 4px 0 8px; }

.ds-river-header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 5px 4px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 6px; }
.ds-river-header:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
.ds-chevron { display: inline-block; transition: transform 0.15s ease; font-size: 10px; width: 10px; color: var(--c-text-mute); }
.ds-river-node.open > .ds-river-header .ds-chevron { transform: rotate(90deg); }
.ds-river-name { flex: 1; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 500; color: var(--c-text); overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.ds-river-count { font-size: 10px; color: var(--c-text-mute); background: var(--c-panel); border-radius: 5px; padding: 1px 6px; }
.ds-dam-list { max-height: 0; overflow: hidden; padding-left: 18px; }
.ds-river-node.open .ds-dam-list { max-height: 1000px; }
.ds-dam-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 3px 4px; font-size: 11.5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 5px; }
.ds-dam-row:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
.ds-dot { width: 7px; height: 7px; border-radius: 50%; flex-shrink: 0; }
.ds-dot-cod { background: var(--c-primary); }
.ds-dot-construction { background: var(--c-accent); }
.ds-dot-planned { background: #93A6AC; }
.ds-noloc { color: #93A6AC; font-style: italic; }

.gis-basemap-row { display: flex; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.gis-basemap-row button {
  font-size: 11px; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--c-border); background: var(--c-panel);
  cursor: pointer; color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-basemap-row button.active { background: var(--c-secondary); color: #fff; border-color: var(--c-secondary); }

.gis-legend-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12.5px; padding: 3px 0; color: var(--c-text); }
.gis-legend-line { width: 20px; border-top: 3px solid var(--c-secondary); }

/* ---------------- Right "About" panel ---------------- */
#gis-right-panel {
  position: fixed; top: 64px; right: 0; bottom: 0; width: 360px; z-index: 10005;
  background: var(--c-bg); box-shadow: var(--shadow); border-top-left-radius: var(--radius); border-bottom-left-radius: var(--radius);
  transform: translateX(100%); transition: transform 0.25s ease; overflow-y: auto; padding: 20px;
}
#gis-right-panel.open { transform: translateX(0); }
#gis-right-panel h2 { margin-top: 0; font-size: 17px; color: var(--c-text); }
#gis-right-panel h3 { font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--c-primary); margin: 18px 0 6px; }
#gis-right-panel p, #gis-right-panel li { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--c-text-mute); line-height: 1.55; }
#gis-right-panel ul { padding-left: 18px; margin: 4px 0; }
#gis-right-panel .gis-panel-close { float: right; cursor: pointer; font-size: 15px; color: var(--c-text-mute); }

/* ---------------- Column picker panel ---------------- */
#ds-columns-panel {
  display: none; position: fixed; top: 64px; left: 320px; bottom: 0; width: 260px; z-index: 10006;
  background: var(--c-bg); box-shadow: var(--shadow); border-top-right-radius: var(--radius); border-bottom-right-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 14px; overflow-y: auto;
}
#ds-columns-panel.open { display: block; }
#ds-columns-panel h4 { margin: 12px 0 4px; font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--c-primary); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .03em; }
#ds-columns-panel h4:first-child { margin-top: 0; }
#ds-columns-panel label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; font-size: 12px; padding: 3px 0; color: var(--c-text); cursor: pointer; }
#ds-columns-panel .ds-col-close { float: right; cursor: pointer; font-size: 14px; color: var(--c-text-mute); }

/* ---------------- Table view modal ---------------- */
#ds-table-modal { display: none; position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 10020; background: rgba(11,43,60,0.55); }
#ds-table-modal.open { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-box {
  background: #fff; width: min(1100px, 92vw); height: min(720px, 88vh); border-radius: var(--radius);
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);
}
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 14px 18px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--c-border); }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-header h3 { margin: 0; font-size: 15px; color: var(--c-text); }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-close { cursor: pointer; font-size: 18px; color: var(--c-text-mute); background: none; border: none; }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-body { flex: 1; overflow: auto; padding: 0 18px 18px; }
#ds-table-modal table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 12px; }
#ds-table-modal th, #ds-table-modal td { border: 1px solid var(--c-border); padding: 5px 8px; text-align: left; white-space: nowrap; }
#ds-table-modal th { background: var(--c-panel); position: sticky; top: 0; cursor: pointer; color: var(--c-text); }
#ds-table-modal th:hover { background: #ecf1f0; }
#ds-table-modal tr:nth-child(even) td { background: #fafcfb; }

/* ---------------- Dam markers ---------------- */
.ds-dam-icon { filter: drop-shadow(0 2px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)); }

@media (max-width: 760px) {
  #gis-left-sidebar, #ds-columns-panel { width: 88vw; }
  #gis-map-wrap.sidebar-open { left: 0; }
  #gis-right-panel { width: 88vw; }
}
</style>
    
<div id="gis-topnav">
  <div class="nav-left">
    <button id="nav-layers-btn"><span class="icon">&#9776;</span> <span data-i18n="layers">Layers</span></button>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-title" data-i18n="title">Laos Hydropower Dams Map</div>
  <div class="nav-right">
    <button id="nav-about-btn"><span class="icon">&#8505;</span> <span data-i18n="about">About Project</span></button>
    <button id="nav-lang-btn"><span class="icon">&#127760;</span> <span id="nav-lang-label">EN</span></button>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="gis-map-wrap" class="sidebar-open"></div>

<div id="gis-left-sidebar" class="open">
  <div class="gis-card gis-search-card">
    <input type="text" id="ds-search" data-i18n-ph="search" placeholder="Search infrastructure...">
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="hydro">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="hydro">
      <span data-i18n="hydropower">Hydropower</span>
      <span class="gis-accordion-chevron">&#9656;</span>
    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <div class="gis-toolbar">
          <button id="ds-select-all" data-i18n="select_all">Select all</button>
          <button id="ds-select-none" data-i18n="select_none">Select none</button>
        </div>
        <div class="gis-toolbar">
          <button id="ds-columns-btn" data-i18n="columns">Columns</button>
          <button id="ds-table-btn" data-i18n="table_view">Table view</button>
        </div>
        <div class="gis-toolbar" style="position:relative;">
          <button id="ds-download" class="primary" data-i18n="download">&#8681; Download</button>
          <div class="ds-menu" id="ds-download-menu" style="display:none;position:absolute;top:100%;left:0;right:0;margin-top:4px;background:#fff;border:1px solid var(--c-border);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:var(--shadow);z-index:10021;overflow:hidden;">
            <button data-fmt="csv" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:8px 10px;font-size:12px;border:none;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;">CSV (.csv)</button>
            <button data-fmt="xlsx" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:8px 10px;font-size:12px;border:none;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;">Excel (.xlsx)</button>
            <button data-fmt="json" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:8px 10px;font-size:12px;border:none;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;">JSON (.json)</button>
            <button data-fmt="dict" style="display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;padding:8px 10px;font-size:12px;border:none;background:#fff;cursor:pointer;" data-i18n="dictionary">Data dictionary (metadata)</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="gis-stats" id="ds-stats">-- selected</div>
        <div class="gis-basemap-row" id="ds-basemap-row"></div>
        <div id="ds-tree"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="transmission">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="transmission">
      <span data-i18n="transmission">Transmission Lines</span>
      <span class="gis-accordion-chevron">&#9656;</span>
    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <label class="gis-layer-toggle-row">
          <input type="checkbox" id="grid-layer-toggle" checked>
          <span class="gis-layer-swatch" style="background:#6A1B9A;"></span>
          <span data-i18n="grid_label">Transmission grid lines</span>
          <span class="gis-layer-count" id="grid-layer-count"></span>
        </label>
        <div class="gis-placeholder" data-i18n="grid_note">Geometry only — the source shapefile had no attribute table (no voltage/line-name data).</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="substations">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="substations">
      <span data-i18n="substations">Substations</span>
      <span class="gis-accordion-chevron">&#9656;</span>
    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <label class="gis-layer-toggle-row">
          <input type="checkbox" id="substation-layer-toggle" checked>
          <span class="gis-layer-swatch" style="background:#6A1B9A; border-radius:2px;"></span>
          <span data-i18n="sub_label">Substations</span>
          <span class="gis-layer-count" id="substation-layer-count"></span>
        </label>
        <div class="gis-placeholder" data-i18n="sub_note">Locations only — no attribute table (name/voltage) in the source shapefile.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="rivers">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="rivers">
      <span data-i18n="rivers">River flow paths</span>
      <span class="gis-accordion-chevron">&#9656;</span>
    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <label class="gis-layer-toggle-row">
          <input type="checkbox" id="river-layer-toggle" checked>
          <span class="gis-layer-swatch" style="background:#29B6F6;"></span>
          <span data-i18n="river_label">River paths</span>
          <span class="gis-layer-count" id="river-layer-count"></span>
        </label>
        <div class="gis-placeholder" data-i18n="river_note">Partial coverage: traced paths for 36 of 130 dams (from Man_Rivers.kml). A wider river network file was referenced but not received — send it over and this can be filled in.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="legend">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="legend">
      <span data-i18n="legend">Legend</span>
      <span class="gis-accordion-chevron">&#9656;</span>
    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="ds-dot ds-dot-cod"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_cod">Commissioned</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="ds-dot ds-dot-construction"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_constr">Under construction</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="ds-dot ds-dot-planned"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_planned">Planned / other</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="gis-legend-line"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_river">River</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><svg width="16" height="16"><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6" fill="none" stroke="#16262E" stroke-width="1.6"/></svg> <span data-i18n="leg_hobo">Reported (Hobomaps)</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><svg width="16" height="16"><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6" fill="none" stroke="#16262E" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-dasharray="2,2"/></svg> <span data-i18n="leg_calc">Calculated (e.g. Google Earth)</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="gis-right-panel">
  <span class="gis-panel-close" id="gis-right-close">&#10005;</span>
  <h2 data-i18n="about_title">About this Project</h2>
  <h3>Interactive Clean Energy Map</h3>
  <p>The Interactive Clean Energy Map is a web-based Geographic Information System (Web GIS) developed to improve access to geospatial information related to clean energy in the Lao People's Democratic Republic. The platform provides an intuitive and interactive environment for exploring spatial datasets, supporting data visualization, and promoting informed decision-making in the clean energy sector.</p>
  <p>Designed with accessibility and usability in mind, the application enables users to explore geographic information through an interactive map interface, helping researchers, policymakers, development partners, students, and the public better understand the distribution and characteristics of clean energy resources and infrastructure across the country.</p>
  <h3>Key Features</h3>
  <ul>
    <li>Interactive web map with zoom and navigation tools</li>
    <li>Search functionality for locations and map features</li>
    <li>Layer management for multiple spatial datasets</li>
    <li>Pop-up information displaying feature attributes</li>
    <li>Responsive interface for desktop and mobile devices</li>
    <li>Integration of geospatial data prepared using open-source GIS technologies</li>
  </ul>
  <h3>Project Objective</h3>
  <p>This application aims to demonstrate how web-based geospatial technologies can support clean energy planning, research, and sustainable development. By transforming spatial data into an accessible digital platform, the project contributes to greater transparency, knowledge sharing, and evidence-based decision-making within Lao PDR's energy sector.</p>
  <h3>Acknowledgement</h3>
  <p>This project was developed as part of the Clean Energy Internship Programme organized by EnergyLab Asia and supported through funding from Australian Aid. The platform reflects the collaborative efforts to strengthen digital innovation, geospatial capacity, and clean energy knowledge in the Lao People's Democratic Republic.</p>
  <h3>Technologies</h3>
  <ul>
    <li>HTML5</li><li>CSS3</li><li>JavaScript</li><li>Leaflet.js</li><li>GeoJSON</li><li>OpenStreetMap</li><li>QGIS</li>
  </ul>
  <p>Thank you for visiting the Interactive Clean Energy Map. We hope this platform serves as a valuable resource for advancing clean energy knowledge and sustainable development in Lao PDR.</p>
</div>

<div id="ds-columns-panel">
  <span class="ds-col-close" id="ds-col-close">&#10005;</span>
  <div id="ds-col-body"></div>
</div>

<div id="ds-table-modal">
  <div class="ds-modal-box">
    <div class="ds-modal-header">
      <h3 data-i18n="table_title">Dam data table</h3>
      <button class="ds-modal-close" id="ds-table-close">&#10005;</button>
    </div>
    <div class="ds-modal-body">
      <table id="ds-table"><thead></thead><tbody></tbody></table>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

"REF.LINK.HOBO", "topic": "Reference", "unit": null, "description": "Source link on hobomaps.org used to verify plant location."}, "Operator Website": {"code": "REF.LINK.OPWEB", "topic": "Reference", "unit": null, "description": "Operator or project website."}, "Note": {"code": "REF.NOTE", "topic": "Reference", "unit": null, "description": "Free-text analyst notes."}};
  const ORIGINAL_COLUMNS = ["Name", "Dam_ID", "Type", "Status", "Power Output [MW]", "Rated flow [m3/s]", "Design head [m]", "Median head (m)", "Turbine efficiency", "Dam Total Storage [Mm3]", "Dam Min [masl]", "Full Level [masl]", "Dam Active storage [Mm3]", "Dam Average Inflow [m3/s]", "Dam Catchment Area [km2]", "EDL Capacity [MW]", "IPP Capacity [MW]", "Generation (GWh per year)", "EDL Production [GWh]", "IPP Production [GWh]", "Power House Latitude (x)", "Power House Longitude (y)", "Dam Latitude (x)", "Dam Longitude (y)", "Intake Latitude (x)", "Intake Longitude (y)", "Turbine type", "Number of Units", "Units as a list", "Region", "River", "Owner", "Start Year", "Alternative Generator names (comma separated)", "Hobomaps Link", "Operator Website", "Note"];
  const MAP_VAR = "map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292";
  const BASEMAP_VARS = {"satellite": "tile_layer_4e46bd493e5f5a93f21144983967e94b", "labels": "tile_layer_654df38a3ab7cae995e1eb246ca03d25", "terrain": "tile_layer_1832f18d9c15b42aaf5557abcad42dba", "street": "tile_layer_683da0d1fcf434057dd8a8fd9b3fd9f4", "light": "tile_layer_effc1c11e4a63c05b0b80d6eddfb1f5a", "dark": "tile_layer_45f7ca1979675bd29ff002d3ba5aeaa1"};
  const OVERLAY_VARS = {};
  const I18N = {"en": {"layers": "Layers", "title": "Laos Hydropower Dams Map", "about": "About Project", "hydropower": "Hydropower", "transmission": "Transmission Lines", "substations": "Substations", "legend": "Legend", "select_all": "Select all", "select_none": "Select none", "columns": "Columns", "table_view": "Table view", "download": "\u2b07 Download", "dictionary": "Data dictionary (metadata)", "no_data": "No transmission line data available yet.", "no_data_sub": "No substation data available yet.", "leg_cod": "Commissioned", "leg_constr": "Under construction", "leg_planned": "Planned / other", "leg_river": "River", "leg_hobo": "Reported (Hobomaps)", "leg_calc": "Calculated (e.g. Google Earth)", "about_title": "About this Project", "table_title": "Dam data table", "search": "Search infrastructure...", "rivers": "River flow paths", "grid_label": "Transmission grid lines", "sub_label": "Substations", "river_label": "River paths", "grid_note": "Geometry only \u2014 the source shapefile had no attribute table (no voltage/line-name data).", "sub_note": "Locations only \u2014 no attribute table (name/voltage) in the source shapefile.", "river_note": "Partial coverage: traced paths for 36 of 130 dams. A wider river network file was referenced but not received."}, "lo": {"layers": "Layers", "title": "Laos Hydropower Dams Map", "about": "About Project", "hydropower": "Hydropower", "transmission": "Transmission Lines", "substations": "Substations", "legend": "Legend", "select_all": "Select all", "select_none": "Select none", "columns": "Columns", "table_view": "Table view", "download": "\u2b07 Download", "dictionary": "Data dictionary (metadata)", "no_data": "No transmission line data available yet.", "no_data_sub": "No substation data available yet.", "leg_cod": "Commissioned", "leg_constr": "Under construction", "leg_planned": "Planned / other", "leg_river": "River", "leg_hobo": "Reported (Hobomaps)", "leg_calc": "Calculated (e.g. Google Earth)", "about_title": "About this Project", "table_title": "Dam data table", "search": "Search infrastructure...", "rivers": "ເສັ້ນທາງນ້ຳ", "grid_label": "ສາຍສົ່ງໄຟຟ້າ", "sub_label": "ສະຖານີໄຟຟ້າຍ່ອຍ", "river_label": "ເສັ້ນທາງນ້ຳ", "grid_note": "ມີແຕ່ຂໍ້ມູນຮູບຮ່າງ — ບໍ່ມີຕາຕະລາງຄຸນສົມບັດ (ແຮງດັນ/ຊື່ສາຍ) ໃນໄຟລ໌ຕົ້ນສະບັບ.", "sub_note": "ມີແຕ່ຕຳແໜ່ງ — ບໍ່ມີຕາຕະລາງຄຸນສົມບັດ (ຊື່/ແຮງດັນ) ໃນໄຟລ໌ຕົ້ນສະບັບ.", "river_note": "ຄອບຄຸມບາງສ່ວນ: ມີເສັ້ນທາງສະເພາະ 36 ຈາກ 130 ເຂື່ອນ."}};

  const CORE_FIELDS = ["Name", "Type", "Status", "Power Output [MW]", "River", "Region", "Owner", "Start Year"];
  let activeFields = new Set(CORE_FIELDS);
  let lang = "en";

  function getMap() { return window[MAP_VAR]; }
  function getLayer(v) { return window[v]; }

  const state = { checked: new Set(DAM_DATA.map(d => d.name)), markers: new Map() };
  let damLayer = null;

  // ---------------- i18n ----------------
  function applyI18n() {
    const dict = I18N[lang];
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n]").forEach(el => {
      const key = el.dataset.i18n;
      if (dict[key]) el.textContent = dict[key];
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-i18n-ph]").forEach(el => {
      const key = el.dataset.i18nPh;
      if (dict[key]) el.setAttribute("placeholder", dict[key]);
    });
    document.getElementById("nav-lang-label").textContent = lang === "en" ? "EN" : "LO";
  }
  document.getElementById("nav-lang-btn").addEventListener("click", () => {
    lang = lang === "en" ? "lo" : "en";
    applyI18n();
  });

  // ---------------- Nav toggles ----------------
  const leftSidebar = document.getElementById("gis-left-sidebar");
  const mapWrap = document.getElementById("gis-map-wrap");
  document.getElementById("nav-layers-btn").addEventListener("click", () => {
    leftSidebar.classList.toggle("open");
    mapWrap.classList.toggle("sidebar-open", leftSidebar.classList.contains("open"));
    setTimeout(() => { const map = getMap(); if (map) map.invalidateSize(); }, 260);
  });
  const rightPanel = document.getElementById("gis-right-panel");
  document.getElementById("nav-about-btn").addEventListener("click", () => rightPanel.classList.toggle("open"));
  document.getElementById("gis-right-close").addEventListener("click", () => rightPanel.classList.remove("open"));

  // ---------------- Accordion (Layers sections) ----------------
  document.querySelectorAll(".gis-accordion-header").forEach(header => {
    header.addEventListener("click", () => {
      header.closest(".gis-accordion").classList.toggle("open");
    });
  });

  // ---------------- Basemap switcher ----------------
  const BASEMAP_LABELS = [
    ["satellite", "Satellite"], ["terrain", "Terrain"], ["street", "Street"], ["light", "Light"], ["dark", "Dark"]
  ];
  function setBasemap(key) {
    const map = getMap();
    if (!map) return;
    ["satellite", "terrain", "street", "light", "dark"].forEach(k => {
      const layer = getLayer(BASEMAP_VARS[k]);
      if (layer && map.hasLayer(layer)) map.removeLayer(layer);
    });
    const layer = getLayer(BASEMAP_VARS[key]);
    if (layer) layer.addTo(map);
    const labelsLayer = getLayer(BASEMAP_VARS.labels);
    if (labelsLayer && !map.hasLayer(labelsLayer)) labelsLayer.addTo(map);
    document.querySelectorAll("#ds-basemap-row button").forEach(b => b.classList.toggle("active", b.dataset.key === key));
  }
  function buildBasemapRow() {
    const row = document.getElementById("ds-basemap-row");
    row.innerHTML = BASEMAP_LABELS.map(([k, label]) =>
      `<button data-key="${k}" class="${k === 'satellite' ? 'active' : ''}">${label}</button>`
    ).join("");
    row.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(b => b.addEventListener("click", () => setBasemap(b.dataset.key)));
  }

  // ---------------- Dam icon: map-pin with lightning bolt, colored by status ----------------
  function statusColor(d) {
    if (d.status_class === "cod") return "#2E7D32";
    if (d.status_class === "construction") return "#FB8C00";
    return "#93A6AC";
  }
  function damIcon(d) {
    const fill = statusColor(d);
    const dash = d.source_class === "hobo" ? "" : 'stroke-dasharray="2,1.5"';
    const svg = `
      <svg width="30" height="42" viewBox="0 0 30 42" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">
        <path d="M15 41 C15 41 27 25.5 27 15 C27 7 21.6 1.5 15 1.5 C8.4 1.5 3 7 3 15 C3 25.5 15 41 15 41 Z"
              fill="${fill}" stroke="#16262E" stroke-width="1.4" ${dash}/>
        <path d="M16.5 6.5 L9.5 17.5 L14 17.5 L12.5 25.5 L20.5 13 L15.5 13 Z" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#16262E" stroke-width="0.6"/>
      </svg>`;
    return L.divIcon({ html: svg, className: "ds-dam-icon", iconSize: [30, 42], iconAnchor: [15, 41], popupAnchor: [0, -38] });
  }

  function popupHtml(d) {
    const rows = [...activeFields].map(col => {
      let val = d.raw[col];
      if (val === null || val === undefined || val === "") val = "N/A";
      const est = d.estimated_fields.includes(col);
      const estMark = est ? ' <span title="Estimated / derived value" style="color:#C88A00;">&#9998;</span>' : "";
      return `<tr><td style="padding:4px;background:#f2f2f2;"><b>${col}</b></td><td style="padding:4px;">${val}${estMark}</td></tr>`;
    }).join("");
    const srcLabel = d.source_class === "hobo" ? "Reported (Hobomaps)" : "Calculated (e.g. Google Earth)";
    return `<div style="font-family:inherit;width:240px;">
      <h4 style="margin-bottom:5px;color:#16262E;">${d.name}</h4>
      <div style="font-size:11px;color:#5B6B73;margin-bottom:4px;">Location source: ${srcLabel}</div>
      <table style="width:100%;font-size:12px;border-collapse:collapse;">${rows}</table>
    </div>`;
  }

  function refreshMap() {
    const map = getMap();
    if (!map) return;
    if (damLayer) map.removeLayer(damLayer);
    damLayer = L.layerGroup();
    state.markers.clear();
    DAM_DATA.forEach(d => {
      if (!d.has_location || !state.checked.has(d.name)) return;
      const marker = L.marker([d.lat, d.lon], { icon: damIcon(d) });
      marker.bindPopup(popupHtml(d));
      marker.bindTooltip(d.name, {direction: "top", offset: [0, -36]});
      marker.addTo(damLayer);
      state.markers.set(d.name, marker);
    });
    damLayer.addTo(map);
    updateStats();
  }

  function updateStats() {
    const total = DAM_DATA.length;
    const checked = state.checked.size;
    const mapped = [...state.checked].filter(n => {
      const d = DAM_DATA.find(x => x.name === n);
      return d && d.has_location;
    }).length;
    document.getElementById("ds-stats").textContent = `${checked} / ${total} selected, ${mapped} shown on map`;
  }

  function buildTree() {
    const tree = document.getElementById("ds-tree");
    tree.innerHTML = "";
    const grouped = new Map();
    DAM_DATA.forEach(d => {
      if (!grouped.has(d.river)) grouped.set(d.river, []);
      grouped.get(d.river).push(d);
    });
    const rivers = [...grouped.keys()].sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.startsWith("Unspecified")) return 1;
      if (b.startsWith("Unspecified")) return -1;
      return a.localeCompare(b);
    });

    rivers.forEach(river => {
      const dams = grouped.get(river).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
      const node = document.createElement("div");
      node.className = "ds-river-node";
      node.dataset.river = river;

      const header = document.createElement("div");
      header.className = "ds-river-header";
      header.innerHTML = `<span class="ds-chevron">&#9656;</span>
        <input type="checkbox" class="ds-river-cb" checked>
        <span class="ds-river-name">${river}</span>
        <span class="ds-river-count">${dams.length}</span>`;
      node.appendChild(header);

      const list = document.createElement("div");
      list.className = "ds-dam-list";
      dams.forEach(d => {
        const row = document.createElement("div");
        row.className = "ds-dam-row";
        row.dataset.name = d.name;
        const dotClass = "ds-dot-" + d.status_class;
        row.innerHTML = `<input type="checkbox" class="ds-dam-cb" checked>
          <span class="ds-dot ${dotClass}"></span>
          <span class="${d.has_location ? '' : 'ds-noloc'}">${d.name}${d.has_location ? '' : ' (no location)'}</span>`;
        const cb = row.querySelector(".ds-dam-cb");
        cb.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (cb.checked) state.checked.add(d.name); else state.checked.delete(d.name);
          syncRiverCheckbox(node, dams);
          refreshMap();
        });
        row.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
          if (e.target === cb) return;
          if (d.has_location) {
            const map = getMap();
            map.flyTo([d.lat, d.lon], Math.max(map.getZoom(), 10));
            const marker = state.markers.get(d.name);
            if (marker) marker.openPopup();
          }
        });
        list.appendChild(row);
      });
      node.appendChild(list);

      header.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        if (e.target.classList.contains("ds-river-cb")) return;
        node.classList.toggle("open");
      });
      const riverCb = header.querySelector(".ds-river-cb");
      riverCb.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const on = riverCb.checked;
        dams.forEach(d => { if (on) state.checked.add(d.name); else state.checked.delete(d.name); });
        list.querySelectorAll(".ds-dam-cb").forEach(cb => cb.checked = on);
        refreshMap();
      });

      tree.appendChild(node);
    });
  }

  function syncRiverCheckbox(node, dams) {
    const allOn = dams.every(d => state.checked.has(d.name));
    const noneOn = dams.every(d => !state.checked.has(d.name));
    const cb = node.querySelector(".ds-river-cb");
    cb.checked = allOn;
    cb.indeterminate = !allOn && !noneOn;
  }

  document.getElementById("ds-search").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
    const term = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    document.querySelectorAll(".ds-river-node").forEach(node => {
      const river = node.dataset.river.toLowerCase();
      const rows = node.querySelectorAll(".ds-dam-row");
      let anyMatch = river.includes(term);
      rows.forEach(row => {
        const match = term === "" || row.dataset.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) || river.includes(term);
        row.style.display = match ? "" : "none";
        if (match) anyMatch = true;
      });
      node.style.display = anyMatch ? "" : "none";
      if (term !== "" && anyMatch) node.classList.add("open");
    });
    if (term !== "") document.querySelector('.gis-accordion[data-section="hydro"]').classList.add("open");
  });

  document.getElementById("ds-select-all").addEventListener("click", () => {
    DAM_DATA.forEach(d => state.checked.add(d.name));
    document.querySelectorAll(".ds-dam-cb, .ds-river-cb").forEach(cb => { cb.checked = true; cb.indeterminate = false; });
    refreshMap();
  });
  document.getElementById("ds-select-none").addEventListener("click", () => {
    state.checked.clear();
    document.querySelectorAll(".ds-dam-cb, .ds-river-cb").forEach(cb => { cb.checked = false; cb.indeterminate = false; });
    refreshMap();
  });

  // ---------------- Column picker ----------------
  function buildColumnPicker() {
    const body = document.getElementById("ds-col-body");
    const byTopic = new Map();
    ORIGINAL_COLUMNS.forEach(col => {
      const topic = (FIELD_METADATA[col] && FIELD_METADATA[col].topic) || "Other";
      if (!byTopic.has(topic)) byTopic.set(topic, []);
      byTopic.get(topic).push(col);
    });
    let html = "";
    byTopic.forEach((cols, topic) => {
      html += `<h4>${topic}</h4>`;
      cols.forEach(col => {
        const checked = activeFields.has(col) ? "checked" : "";
        const isCore = CORE_FIELDS.includes(col);
        html += `<label><input type="checkbox" class="ds-field-cb" data-col="${col.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}" ${checked} ${isCore ? "disabled" : ""}> ${col}</label>`;
      });
    });
    body.innerHTML = html;
    body.querySelectorAll(".ds-field-cb").forEach(cb => {
      cb.addEventListener("change", () => {
        const col = cb.dataset.col;
        if (cb.checked) activeFields.add(col); else activeFields.delete(col);
        refreshMap();
        if (document.getElementById("ds-table-modal").classList.contains("open")) buildTable();
      });
    });
  }
  document.getElementById("ds-columns-btn").addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("ds-columns-panel").classList.add("open");
  });
  document.getElementById("ds-col-close").addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("ds-columns-panel").classList.remove("open");
  });

  // ---------------- Table view ----------------
  let sortState = { col: null, dir: 1 };
  function buildTable() {
    const cols = [...activeFields];
    const thead = document.querySelector("#ds-table thead");
    const tbody = document.querySelector("#ds-table tbody");
    thead.innerHTML = "<tr>" + cols.map(c => `<th data-col="${c.replace(/"/g, '&quot;')}">${c}</th>`).join("") + "</tr>";
    let rows = DAM_DATA.filter(d => state.checked.has(d.name));
    if (sortState.col) {
      rows = rows.slice().sort((a, b) => {
        const av = a.raw[sortState.col], bv = b.raw[sortState.col];
        if (av == null && bv == null) return 0;
        if (av == null) return 1;
        if (bv == null) return -1;
        if (typeof av === "number" && typeof bv === "number") return (av - bv) * sortState.dir;
        return String(av).localeCompare(String(bv)) * sortState.dir;
      });
    }
    tbody.innerHTML = rows.map(d => "<tr>" + cols.map(c => {
      let v = d.raw[c];
      if (v === null || v === undefined || v === "") v = "";
      return `<td>${v}</td>`;
    }).join("") + "</tr>").join("");
    thead.querySelectorAll("th").forEach(th => {
      th.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const col = th.dataset.col;
        sortState.dir = (sortState.col === col) ? -sortState.dir : 1;
        sortState.col = col;
        buildTable();
      });
    });
  }
  document.getElementById("ds-table-btn").addEventListener("click", () => {
    buildTable();
    document.getElementById("ds-table-modal").classList.add("open");
  });
  document.getElementById("ds-table-close").addEventListener("click", () => {
    document.getElementById("ds-table-modal").classList.remove("open");
  });
  document.getElementById("ds-table-modal").addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    if (e.target.id === "ds-table-modal") e.currentTarget.classList.remove("open");
  });

  // ---------------- Downloads ----------------
  function selectedRawRows() { return DAM_DATA.filter(d => state.checked.has(d.name)).map(d => d.raw); }
  function triggerDownload(blob, filename) {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url; a.download = filename;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }
  function toCSV(rows, cols) {
    const esc = (v) => {
      if (v === null || v === undefined) return "";
      const s = String(v).replace(/"/g, '""');
      return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s}"` : s;
    };
    const lines = [cols.map(esc).join(",")];
    rows.forEach(r => lines.push(cols.map(c => esc(r[c])).join(",")));
    return lines.join("\n");
  }
  function downloadCSV() {
    const rows = selectedRawRows();
    if (!rows.length) { alert("No dams selected."); return; }
    triggerDownload(new Blob([toCSV(rows, ORIGINAL_COLUMNS)], {type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;"}), "laos_dams_selected.csv");
  }
  function downloadXLSX() {
    const rows = selectedRawRows();
    if (!rows.length) { alert("No dams selected."); return; }
    const ws = XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows, { header: ORIGINAL_COLUMNS });
    const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
    XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Hydro Plant Data");
    XLSX.writeFile(wb, "laos_dams_selected.xlsx");
  }
  function downloadJSON() {
    const rows = selectedRawRows();
    if (!rows.length) { alert("No dams selected."); return; }
    triggerDownload(new Blob([JSON.stringify(rows, null, 2)], {type: "application/json;charset=utf-8;"}), "laos_dams_selected.json");
  }
  function downloadDictionary() {
    const cols = ["code", "field", "topic", "unit", "description"];
    const rows = ORIGINAL_COLUMNS.map(col => {
      const meta = FIELD_METADATA[col] || {};
      return { code: meta.code || "", field: col, topic: meta.topic || "", unit: meta.unit || "", description: meta.description || "" };
    });
    triggerDownload(new Blob([toCSV(rows, cols)], {type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;"}), "laos_dams_data_dictionary.csv");
  }
  const downloadBtn = document.getElementById("ds-download");
  const downloadMenu = document.getElementById("ds-download-menu");
  downloadBtn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    downloadMenu.style.display = downloadMenu.style.display === "block" ? "none" : "block";
  });
  document.addEventListener("click", () => downloadMenu.style.display = "none");
  downloadMenu.querySelectorAll("button").forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      downloadMenu.style.display = "none";
      const fmt = btn.dataset.fmt;
      if (fmt === "csv") downloadCSV();
      else if (fmt === "xlsx") downloadXLSX();
      else if (fmt === "json") downloadJSON();
      else if (fmt === "dict") downloadDictionary();
    });
  });

  function init() {
    if (!getMap()) { setTimeout(init, 100); return; }
    buildTree();
    buildColumnPicker();
    buildBasemapRow();
    refreshMap();
    applyI18n();
    setTimeout(() => getMap().invalidateSize(), 50);
  }
  window.addEventListener("load", init);
})();
</script>
    
            <div class="folium-map" id="map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292" ></div>
        
</body>
<script>
    
    
            var map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292 = L.map(
                "map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292",
                {
                    center: [18.5, 103.5],
                    crs: L.CRS.EPSG3857,
                    ...{
  "zoom": 7,
  "zoomControl": false,
  "preferCanvas": false,
}

                }
            );
            L.control.scale().addTo(map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292);

            

        
    
            var tile_layer_4e46bd493e5f5a93f21144983967e94b = L.tileLayer(
                "https://server.arcgisonline.com/ArcGIS/rest/services/World_Imagery/MapServer/tile/{z}/{y}/{x}",
                {
  "minZoom": 0,
  "maxZoom": 18,
  "maxNativeZoom": 18,
  "noWrap": false,
  "attribution": "Esri World Imagery",
  "subdomains": "abc",
  "detectRetina": false,
  "tms": false,
  "opacity": 1,
}

            );

{"name": "P-Nam Chea", "coords": [[19.0550692131604, 103.5132990980575], [19.04938927609238, 103.5065888590237], [19.04775640065857, 103.5048023950346], [19.04734456224836, 103.5045369090761], [19.04712627884535, 103.5040539819506]]}, {"name": "P-Houay Lampan Gnai", "coords": [[15.33478123024202, 106.5313988235993], [15.34120528055863, 106.5549062429535], [15.34925965763038, 106.5721371061114], [15.3564013355486, 106.5863669308587], [15.35600410240067, 106.5868053390737], [15.3556042728926, 106.5868046754477], [15.35517412244642, 106.586566222978], [15.35500593886134, 106.5865143677378]]}];

  function initExtraLayers() {
    const map = window["map_96e4324bcd8babeea132bb9d3fc56292"];
    if (!map) { setTimeout(initExtraLayers, 100); return; }

    // ---------------- Transmission grid ----------------
    const gridLayer = L.layerGroup();
    GRID_LINES.forEach(coords => {
      // coords is either [[lat,lon],...] (LineString) or [[[lat,lon],...],...] (MultiLineString)
      const isMulti = Array.isArray(coords[0][0]);
      const parts = isMulti ? coords : [coords];
      parts.forEach(part => {
        L.polyline(part, { color: "#6A1B9A", weight: 2, opacity: 0.85 })
          .bindPopup('<div class="gis-grid-popup"><b>Transmission line segment</b><br>No attribute data in source file.</div>')
          .addTo(gridLayer);
      });
    });
    gridLayer.addTo(map);
    const gridCountEl = document.getElementById("grid-layer-count");
    if (gridCountEl) gridCountEl.textContent = GRID_LINES.length + " segments";

    // ---------------- Substations ----------------
    const subIcon = L.divIcon({
      html: '<div style="width:12px;height:12px;background:#6A1B9A;border:1.5px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #6A1B9A;transform:rotate(45deg);"></div>',
      className: "", iconSize: [12, 12], iconAnchor: [6, 6],
    });
    const subLayer = L.layerGroup();
    SUB_POINTS.forEach((pt, i) => {
      L.marker(pt, { icon: subIcon })
        .bindPopup('<div class="gis-sub-popup"><b>Substation ' + (i + 1) + '</b><br>' + pt[0].toFixed(4) + ', ' + pt[1].toFixed(4) + '<br>No attribute data in source file.</div>')
        .bindTooltip("Substation " + (i + 1))
        .addTo(subLayer);
    });
    subLayer.addTo(map);
    const subCountEl = document.getElementById("substation-layer-count");
    if (subCountEl) subCountEl.textContent = SUB_POINTS.length;

    // ---------------- River flow paths ----------------
    const riverLayer = L.layerGroup();
    RIVER_FEATURES.forEach(f => {
      const isMulti = Array.isArray(f.coords[0][0]);
      const parts = isMulti ? f.coords : [f.coords];
      const name = (f.name || "").replace(/^P-/, "");
      parts.forEach(part => {
        L.polyline(part, { color: "#29B6F6", weight: 2, opacity: 0.85, dashArray: "4, 4" })
          .bindPopup('<div class="gis-river-popup"><b>' + name + '</b><br>Traced river path</div>')
          .addTo(riverLayer);
      });
    });
    riverLayer.addTo(map);
    const riverCountEl = document.getElementById("river-layer-count");
    if (riverCountEl) riverCountEl.textContent = RIVER_FEATURES.length + " paths";

    // ---------------- Toggle wiring ----------------
    function wireToggle(checkboxId, layer) {
      const cb = document.getElementById(checkboxId);
      if (!cb) return;
      cb.addEventListener("change", () => {
        if (cb.checked) map.addLayer(layer); else map.removeLayer(layer);
      });
    }
    wireToggle("grid-layer-toggle", gridLayer);
    wireToggle("substation-layer-toggle", subLayer);
    wireToggle("river-layer-toggle", riverLayer);
  }

  ready(initExtraLayers);
})();
</script>

</html>}
.gis-accordion-header {
  display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 13px 14px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-accordion-header:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
.gis-accordion-chevron { transition: transform 0.15s ease; color: var(--c-text-mute); font-size: 11px; }
.gis-accordion.open .gis-accordion-chevron { transform: rotate(90deg); }
.gis-accordion-body { max-height: 0; overflow: hidden; transition: max-height 0.2s ease; }
.gis-accordion.open .gis-accordion-body { max-height: 2000px; }
.gis-accordion-body-inner { padding: 0 14px 14px; }
.gis-placeholder { font-size: 12px; color: var(--c-text-mute); padding: 6px 0 2px; font-style: italic; }
.gis-layer-toggle-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--c-text); padding: 4px 2px; cursor: pointer; }
.gis-layer-swatch { width: 16px; height: 3px; border-radius: 2px; flex-shrink: 0; }
.gis-layer-count { margin-left: auto; font-size: 10.5px; color: var(--c-text-mute); background: #fff; border: 1px solid var(--c-border); border-radius: 20px; padding: 1px 8px; }

.gis-toolbar { display: flex; gap: 6px; margin-bottom: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
.gis-toolbar button {
  flex: 1 1 auto; font-size: 11px; padding: 6px 8px; border-radius: 8px; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap;
  border: 1px solid var(--c-border); background: var(--c-panel); color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-toolbar button:hover { background: #ecf1f0; }
.gis-toolbar button.primary { background: var(--c-primary); color: #fff; border-color: var(--c-primary); }
.gis-toolbar button.primary:hover { background: var(--c-primary-dark); }
.gis-stats { font-size: 11px; color: var(--c-text-mute); margin: 4px 0 8px; }

.ds-river-header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 5px 4px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 6px; }
.ds-river-header:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
.ds-chevron { display: inline-block; transition: transform 0.15s ease; font-size: 10px; width: 10px; color: var(--c-text-mute); }
.ds-river-node.open > .ds-river-header .ds-chevron { transform: rotate(90deg); }
.ds-river-name { flex: 1; font-size: 12px; font-weight: 500; color: var(--c-text); overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
.ds-river-count { font-size: 10px; color: var(--c-text-mute); background: var(--c-panel); border-radius: 5px; padding: 1px 6px; }
.ds-dam-list { max-height: 0; overflow: hidden; padding-left: 18px; }
.ds-river-node.open .ds-dam-list { max-height: 1000px; }
.ds-dam-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 3px 4px; font-size: 11.5px; cursor: pointer; border-radius: 5px; }
.ds-dam-row:hover { background: var(--c-panel); }
.ds-dot { width: 7px; height: 7px; border-radius: 50%; flex-shrink: 0; }
.ds-dot-cod { background: var(--c-primary); }
.ds-dot-construction { background: var(--c-accent); }
.ds-dot-planned { background: #93A6AC; }
.ds-noloc { color: #93A6AC; font-style: italic; }

.gis-basemap-row { display: flex; gap: 6px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 4px; }
.gis-basemap-row button {
  font-size: 11px; padding: 6px 10px; border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--c-border); background: var(--c-panel);
  cursor: pointer; color: var(--c-text);
}
.gis-basemap-row button.active { background: var(--c-secondary); color: #fff; border-color: var(--c-secondary); }

.gis-legend-row { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; font-size: 12.5px; padding: 3px 0; color: var(--c-text); }
.gis-legend-line { width: 20px; border-top: 3px solid var(--c-secondary); }

/* ---------------- Right "About" panel ---------------- */
#gis-right-panel {
  position: fixed; top: 64px; right: 0; bottom: 0; width: 360px; z-index: 10005;
  background: var(--c-bg); box-shadow: var(--shadow); border-top-left-radius: var(--radius); border-bottom-left-radius: var(--radius);
  transform: translateX(100%); transition: transform 0.25s ease; overflow-y: auto; padding: 20px;
}
#gis-right-panel.open { transform: translateX(0); }
#gis-right-panel h2 { margin-top: 0; font-size: 17px; color: var(--c-text); }
#gis-right-panel h3 { font-size: 13.5px; color: var(--c-primary); margin: 18px 0 6px; }
#gis-right-panel p, #gis-right-panel li { font-size: 12.5px; color: var(--c-text-mute); line-height: 1.55; }
#gis-right-panel ul { padding-left: 18px; margin: 4px 0; }
#gis-right-panel .gis-panel-close { float: right; cursor: pointer; font-size: 15px; color: var(--c-text-mute); }

/* ---------------- Column picker panel ---------------- */
#ds-columns-panel {
  display: none; position: fixed; top: 64px; left: 320px; bottom: 0; width: 260px; z-index: 10006;
  background: var(--c-bg); box-shadow: var(--shadow); border-top-right-radius: var(--radius); border-bottom-right-radius: var(--radius);
  padding: 14px; overflow-y: auto;
}
#ds-columns-panel.open { display: block; }
#ds-columns-panel h4 { margin: 12px 0 4px; font-size: 11.5px; color: var(--c-primary); text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .03em; }
#ds-columns-panel h4:first-child { margin-top: 0; }
#ds-columns-panel label { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; font-size: 12px; padding: 3px 0; color: var(--c-text); cursor: pointer; }
#ds-columns-panel .ds-col-close { float: right; cursor: pointer; font-size: 14px; color: var(--c-text-mute); }

/* ---------------- Table view modal ---------------- */
#ds-table-modal { display: none; position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 10020; background: rgba(11,43,60,0.55); }
#ds-table-modal.open { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-box {
  background: #fff; width: min(1100px, 92vw); height: min(720px, 88vh); border-radius: var(--radius);
  display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden; box-shadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);
}
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-header { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; padding: 14px 18px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--c-border); }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-header h3 { margin: 0; font-size: 15px; color: var(--c-text); }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-close { cursor: pointer; font-size: 18px; color: var(--c-text-mute); background: none; border: none; }
#ds-table-modal .ds-modal-body { flex: 1; overflow: auto; padding: 0 18px 18px; }
#ds-table-modal table { border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 12px; }
#ds-table-modal th, #ds-table-modal td { border: 1px solid var(--c-border); padding: 5px 8px; text-align: left; white-space: nowrap; }
#ds-table-modal th { background: var(--c-panel); position: sticky; top: 0; cursor: pointer; color: var(--c-text); }
#ds-table-modal th:hover { background: #ecf1f0; }
#ds-table-modal tr:nth-child(even) td { background: #fafcfb; }

/* ---------------- Dam markers ---------------- */
.ds-dam-icon { filter: drop-shadow(0 2px 3px rgba(0,0,0,0.35)); }

@media (max-width: 760px) {
  #gis-left-sidebar, #ds-columns-panel { width: 88vw; }
  #gis-map-wrap.sidebar-open { left: 0; }
  #gis-right-panel { width: 88vw; }
}
</style>
    
<div id="gis-topnav">
  <div class="nav-left">
    <button id="nav-layers-btn"><span class="icon">&#9776;</span> <span data-i18n="layers">Layers</span></button>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-title" data-i18n="title">Laos Hydropower Dams Map</div>
  <div class="nav-right">
    <button id="nav-about-btn"><span class="icon">&#8505;</span> <span data-i18n="about">About Project</span></button>
    <button id="nav-lang-btn"><span class="icon">&#127760;</span> <span id="nav-lang-label">EN</span></button>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="gis-map-wrap" class="sidebar-open"></div>

<div id="gis-left-sidebar" class="open">
  <div class="gis-card gis-search-card">
    <input type="text" id="ds-search" data-i18n-ph="search" placeholder="Search infrastructure...">
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="hydro">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="hydro">
      <span data-i18n="hydropower">Hydropower</span>
      <span class="gis-accordion-chevron">&#9656;</span>
    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <div class="gis-toolbar">
          <button id="ds-select-all" data-i18n="select_all">Select all</button>
          <button id="ds-select-none" data-i18n="select_none">Select none</button>
        </div>
        <div class="gis-toolbar">
          <button id="ds-columns-btn" data-i18n="columns">Columns</button>
          <button id="ds-table-btn" data-i18n="table_view">Table view</button>
        </div>
        <div class="gis-toolbar" style="position:relative;">
          <button id="ds-download" class="primary" data-i18n="download">&#8681; Download</button>

    </div>

        <label class="gis-layer-toggle-row">
          <input type="checkbox" id="substation-layer-toggle" checked>
          <span class="gis-layer-swatch" style="background:#6A1B9A; border-radius:2px;"></span>
          <span data-i18n="sub_label">Substations</span>
          <span class="gis-layer-count" id="substation-layer-count"></span>
        </label>

      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="rivers">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="rivers">

    </div>
    <div class="gis-accordion-body">
      <div class="gis-accordion-body-inner">
        <label class="gis-layer-toggle-row">
          <input type="checkbox" id="river-layer-toggle" checked>
          <span class="gis-layer-swatch" style="background:#29B6F6;"></span>
          <span data-i18n="river_label">River paths</span>
          <span class="gis-layer-count" id="river-layer-count"></span>
        </label>
        <div class="gis-placeholder" data-i18n="river_note">Partial coverage: traced paths for 36 of 130 dams (from Man_Rivers.kml). A wider river network file was referenced but not received — send it over and this can be filled in.</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="gis-card gis-accordion" data-section="legend">
    <div class="gis-accordion-header" data-toggle="legend">
      <span data-i18n="legend">Legend</span>

    </div>

        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="ds-dot ds-dot-construction"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_constr">Under construction</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="ds-dot ds-dot-planned"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_planned">Planned / other</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><span class="gis-legend-line"></span> <span data-i18n="leg_river">River</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><svg width="16" height="16"><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6" fill="none" stroke="#16262E" stroke-width="1.6"/></svg> <span data-i18n="leg_hobo">Reported (Hobomaps)</span></div>
        <div class="gis-legend-row"><svg width="16" height="16"><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6" fill="none" stroke="#16262E" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-dasharray="2,2"/></svg> <span data-i18n="leg_calc">Calculated (e.g. Google Earth)</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="gis-right-panel">
  <span class="gis-panel-close" id="gis-right-close">&#10005;</span>
  <h2 data-i18n="about_title">About this Project</h2>
  <h3>Interactive Clean Energy Map</h3>
  <p>The Interactive Clean Energy Map is a web-based Geographic Information System (Web GIS) developed to improve access to geospatial information related to clean energy in the Lao People's Democratic Republic. The platform provides an intuitive and interactive environment for exploring spatial datasets, supporting data visualization, and promoting informed decision-making in the clean energy sector.</p>
  <p>Designed with accessibility and usability in mind, the application enables users to explore geographic information through an interactive map interface, helping researchers, policymakers, development partners, students, and the public better understand the distribution and characteristics of clean energy resources and infrastructure across the country.</p>
  <h3>Key Features</h3>
  <ul>
    <li>Interactive web map with zoom and navigation tools</li>
    <li>Search functionality for locations and map features</li>
    <li>Layer management for multiple spatial datasets</li>
    <li>Pop-up information displaying feature attributes</li>
    <li>Responsive interface for desktop and mobile devices</li>
    <li>Integration of geospatial data prepared using open-source GIS technologies</li>
  </ul>
  <h3>Project Objective</h3>
  <p>This application aims to demonstrate how web-based geospatial technologies can support clean energy planning, research, and sustainable development. By transforming spatial data into an accessible digital platform, the project contributes to greater transparency, knowledge sharing, and evidence-based decision-making within Lao PDR's energy sector.</p>
  <h3>Acknowledgement</h3>
  <p>This project was developed as part of the Clean Energy Internship Programme organized by EnergyLab Asia and supported through funding from Australian Aid. The platform reflects the collaborative efforts to strengthen digital innovation, geospatial capacity, and clean energy knowledge in the Lao People's Democratic Republic.</p>
  <h3>Technologies</h3>
  <ul>
    <li>HTML5</li><li>CSS3</li><li>JavaScript</li><li>Leaflet.js</li><li>GeoJSON</li><li>OpenStreetMap</li><li>QGIS</li>
  </ul>
  <p>Thank you for visiting the Interactive Clean Energy Map. We hope this platform serves as a valuable resource for advancing clean energy knowledge and sustainable development in Lao PDR.</p>
</div>
